<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**


How many seconds are there in four minutes?

-  $4$ 
-  $60$ 
-  $460$ 
-  $240$
-  $3600$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">
How many seconds are there in four minutes?
<emptyline>
-  $4$ 
-  $60$ 
-  $460$ 
-  $240$
-  $3600$
</code></pre>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

It is $165 \ \text{minutes}$ before midnight. 
Put one digit into each of the four boxes to show the time on this $24 \ \text{-hour}$ digital clock.


$
\huge {
\boxed {\phantom{2}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{2}}
: 
\boxed {\phantom{1}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{3}}
}
$


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">It is $165 \ \text{minutes}$ before midnight. 
Put one digit into each of the four boxes to show the time on this $24 \ \text{-hour}$ digital clock.
<emptyline>

$
\huge {
\boxed {\phantom{2}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{2}}
: 
\boxed {\phantom{1}} \ 
\boxed {\phantom{3}}
}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24{:}00 - 165 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 120 \ \text {minutes} - 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 24{:}00 - 2 \ \text {hours} \ 45 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 21{:}15
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$21{:}15$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The lengths of four of the Henry Potter films are as follows:

| Henry Potter and the Philosopher's Loan  | $143 \ \text{minutes}$|
|:----------------------------------------:|:--------------:|
| Henry Potter and the Pizza of Azkaban    | $158 \ \text{minutes}$|
| Henry Potter and the Half Asleep Prince  | $144 \ \text{minutes}$|
| Henry Potter and the Dodgy Shadows       | $135 \ \text{minutes}$|

Find the total length of all four films, giving your answer in hours and minutes.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The lengths of four of the Henry Potter films are as follows:
<emptyline>
| Henry Potter and the Philosopher's Loan  | $143 \ \text{minutes}$|
|:----------------------------------------:|:--------------:|
| Henry Potter and the Pizza of Azkaban    | $158 \ \text{minutes}$|
| Henry Potter and the Half Asleep Prince  | $144 \ \text{minutes}$|
| Henry Potter and the Dodgy Shadows       | $135 \ \text{minutes}$|
<emptyline>
Find the total length of all four films, giving your answer in hours and minutes.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total length} &= 143 + 158 + 144 + 135 \\\\
                    &= 580 \text { minutes} \\\\
                    &= 9 \text { hours } 40 \text { minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9 \text { hours } 40 \text { minutes}$

</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Jack runs the $100 \ \text{metres}$  in a time of $15.47 \ \text{seconds}$.
Emily runs the same distance in a time of $13.762 \ \text{seconds}$.

Estimate the difference in their times by rounding each number to the nearest whole number and subtracting.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

Emily runs the same distance in a time of $13.762 \ \text{seconds}$.
<emptyline>
Estimate the difference in their times by rounding each number to the nearest whole number and subtracting.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}              &= 15.47 - 13.762 \\\\ 
                                &\approx 15 - 14 \\\\
                                &= 1 \ \text {second}                        
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$1 \ \text {second}$

</div>

**Question**

I went to watch a three-hour concert but the music was so awful that I only stayed for $15 \%$ of it.
How many minutes did I stay at the concert for?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I went to watch a three-hour concert but the music was so awful that I only stayed for $15 \%$ of it.
How many minutes did I stay at the concert for?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Minutes stayed}              &= 15 \% \ \text {of} \ 3 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 3 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \times 60} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \times 2 \times 10} {5 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                                    &= 3 \times 3 \times 3 \\\\
                                    &= 27 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$27 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                    &= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 3 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {15 \times 3 \times 60} {100} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \times 2 \times 10} {5 \times 2 \times 10} \\\\
                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 3 \times 3 \times 3 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\

$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many $\text{minutes}$ are there between $9.23\ \text{am}$ and $1.06\ \text{pm}$? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many $\text{minutes}$ are there between $9.23\ \text{am}$ and $1.06\ \text{pm}$? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1{:}06 \ \text {pm} - 9{:}23 \ \text {am} \\\\
&= 3 \ \text {hours} \  43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 3 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 43 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
&= 180 + 43 \\\\
&= 223 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$223 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

Petrov downloads and listens to the 'Request Slot' and the 'Re-mix Revenge' immediately after each other. 
He starts listenig at $1952$, what time does he finish?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Duration for Request slot}        &= 17{:}57 - 17{:}48 \\\\
                                        &= 9 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Duration for  Re-mix revenge}     &= 18{:}57 - 18{:}52 \\\\
                                        &= 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text{Finish time}                      &= 19{:}52 + 9  + 5  \\\\
                                        &= 20{:}06
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My journey to school generally takes $40 \ \text{minutes}$. What time shall I leave home if I want to arrive at $8.15 \ \text{a.m}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">My journey to school generally takes $40 \ \text{minutes}$. What time shall I leave home if I want to arrive at $8.15 \ \text{a.m}$?
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
\text{Leaving time}    &= 8{:}15 \ \text{am} - 40 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                       &= 7{:}35 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many seconds in $24 \ \text{hours}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds in $24 \ \text{hours}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&=  24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text{seconds} \\\\
&=  24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$86400  \ \text {seconds}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sun rose at $05{:}43$ and set at $18{:}05$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The sun rose at $05{:}43$ and set at $18{:}05$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 18{:}05 - 5{:}43 \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \ \text{hour} \ 22 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 22 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 720 + 22 \\\\
                                                    &= 742 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$742  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&=12 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 12 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 43200 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sun rose at $05{:}34$ and set at $21{:}15$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The sun rose at $05{:}34$ and set at $21{:}15$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}15 - 5{:}34 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 41 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 41  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 41 \\\\
                                                    &= 941  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$941  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The school day starts at $08{:}55$ and finishes at $15{:}20$. How many minutes long is the school day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The school day starts at $08{:}55$ and finishes at $15{:}20$. How many minutes long is the school day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{School duration}      &= 15{:}20 - 08{:}55 \\\\
                            &= 6 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                            &= 6 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 25  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                            &= 360 + 25 \\\\
                            &= 385  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$385  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many seconds in $24 \ \text{hours}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds in $24 \ \text{hours}?$
</code></pre>

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The sun rose at $05{:}37$ and set at $21{:}10$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The sun rose at $05{:}37$ and set at $21{:}10$. How many minutes were there between sunrise and sunset?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}                                   &= 21{:}10 - 5{:}37 \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \ \text{hour} \ 33 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                                                    &= 15 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 33  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                    &= 900 + 33 \\\\
                                                    &= 933  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$933  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Shanghai in China is $8 \ \text{hours}$ ahead of the time in the UK. If it is $09.00$ in the UK, what time is it in Shanghai?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Shanghai in China is $8 \ \text{hours}$ ahead of the time in the UK. If it is $09.00$ in the UK, what time is it in Shanghai?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>

<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time in Shanghai}     &= 9{:}00 + 8 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                            &= 17{:}00
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$17{:}00$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A coach journey from Plymouth to Exeter leaves at $16.15$ and arrives at $17.36$. How many minutes does the journey take?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A coach journey from Plymouth to Exeter leaves at $16.15$ and arrives at $17.36$. How many minutes does the journey take?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 17{:}36 - 16{:}15 \\\\
                      &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 21 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 21 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 60 + 21 \\\\
                      &= 81  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$81  \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}     &= 17{:}36 - 16{:}15 \\\\
                      &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 21 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                      &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 21 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                      &= 60 + 21 \\\\
                      &= 81  \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many seconds in a day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many seconds in a day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$86400 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1 \ \text{day} \\\\
&= 24 \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 24 \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= 24 \times 3600 \\\\
&= 86400 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$86400 \ \text {seconds}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Train start at station $A$ at $10{:}45 \ \text{am}$ and reaches station $B$ at $13{:}20$. If it stops for $10 \ \text{mins}$ 
in between. How much is the travel time from station $A$ to $B$ if it doesn't stop in between.

-  $2 \ \text{h} \ 25 \ \text{mins}$
-  $3 \ \text{h} \ 40 \ \text{mins}$
-  $2 \ \text{h} \ 35 \ \text{mins}$
-  $4 \ \text{h} \ 20 \ \text{mins}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Train start at station $A$ at $10{:}45 \ \text{am}$ and reaches station $B$ at $13{:}20$. If it stops for $10 \ \text{mins}$ 
in between. How much is the travel time from station $A$ to $B$ if it doesn't stop in between.
<emptyline>
-  $2 \ \text{h} \ 25 \ \text{mins}$
-  $3 \ \text{h} \ 40 \ \text{mins}$
-  $2 \ \text{h} \ 35 \ \text{mins}$
-  $4 \ \text{h} \ 20 \ \text{mins}$
</code></pre>
</div>

\text{Travel Time}  &=  13{:}20 - 10{:}45 - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 35 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 2 \ \text{hour} \ 25 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Question**

A train leaves London at $10.35 \ \text{am}$ and 
arrives in Exeter $3 \ \text{hours}$ and $35 \ \text{minutes}$ later. 
What time does it arrive? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A programme starts at $9{:}15 \ \text{pm}$ and ends at $11{:}06 \ \text{pm}$.
How many minutes did it last? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A programme starts at $9{:}15 \ \text{pm}$ and ends at $11{:}06 \ \text{pm}$.
How many minutes did it last? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Duration} &= 11{:}06 \ \text{pm} - 9{:}15 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 51 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                &= 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + 51 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                &= 60 + 51 \\\\
                &= 111 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$111 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Prasha is swimming lengths at her local swimming pool.

She takes $24 \ \text{seconds}$ to swim a length doing front crawl.

She takes $60 \ \text{seconds}$ to swim a length doing breaststroke.

She swims $3$ lengths of each (so a total of $6$ lengths).

The last length takes an extra $6 \ \text{seconds}$ as she gets tired.

How long did she take to swim all $6$ lengths? Give your answer in minutes and seconds. 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Prasha is swimming lengths at her local swimming pool.
<emptyline>
She takes $24 \ \text{seconds}$ to swim a length doing front crawl.
<emptyline>
She takes $60 \ \text{seconds}$ to swim a length doing breaststroke.
<emptyline>
She swims $3$ lengths of each (so a total of $6$ lengths).
<emptyline>
The last length takes an extra $6 \ \text{seconds}$ as she gets tired.
<emptyline>
How long did she take to swim all $6$ lengths? Give your answer in minutes and seconds. 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total time} &= 3 \times 24 + 3 \times 60 + 6 \\\\
                   &= 72 + 180 + 6 \\\\
                   &= 258 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
                   &= 4 \ \text {minutes} \ 18 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \ \text {minutes} \ 18 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Gina finished her homework at $8{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ having started at $6{:}52 \ \text{pm}$.
How many minutes did she spend on it?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Gina finished her homework at $8{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ having started at $6{:}52 \ \text{pm}$.
How many minutes did she spend on it?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time spent}          &= 8{:}24 \ \text{pm} - 6{:}52 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                              &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 32 \ \text{minutes}   \\\\
                              &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 32  \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 60 + 32 \\\\
                              &= 92 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$92 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many hours are there in $2$ weeks? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

&= 2 \text { weeks} \\\\
&= 14 \text { days} \\\\
&= 14 \times 24 \text { hours} \\\\
&= 336 \text { hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Sanjay is $8 \ \text{years}$ old.

Use the rule to work out how many hours' sleep he needs.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Sanjay is $8 \ \text{years}$ old.
<emptyline>
Use the rule to work out how many hours' sleep he needs.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of hours}   &= \dfrac {30 - 8} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                          &= 11 \ \text {hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$11 \ \text {hours}$

\begin{aligned}
\text {Number of hours}   &= \dfrac {30 - 8} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {22} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                          &= \dfrac {11 \times \cancel {2}} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
                          &= 11 \ \text {hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

**Question**

Lisa is $6 \ \text{years}$ old.

She wakes up every morning at $7 \ \text{am}$.

Use the rule to work out what time she needs to go to sleep.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Lisa is $6 \ \text{years}$ old.

</code></pre>

                           &= 12 \ \text {hours} \\\\
\text {Sleep Time} &= 7 \ \text{am} - 12 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                   &= 7 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Calculate how many seconds there are in $15 \dfrac{1}{2}$ hours.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Calculate how many seconds there are in $15 \dfrac{1}{2}$ hours.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31} {2} \times 60 \times 60 \ \text {seconds} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31 \times 60 \times 60} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31 \times 60 \times 30 \times 2} {2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {31 \times 60 \times 30 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
&= 31 \times 60 \times 30 \\\\
&= 55800 \ \text {seconds}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$55800 \ \text {seconds}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

&= 15 \dfrac {1} {2} \ \text {hours} \\\\

&= \dfrac {31 \times 60 \times 60} {2} \\\\

&= \dfrac {31 \times 60 \times 30 \times \cancel{2}} {\cancel{2}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What time is $1 \ \text{hour}$ $32 \ \text{minutes}$ before $7{:}19 \ \text{pm}$?

- $4{:}42 \ \text{pm}$
- $4{:}52 \ \text{pm}$
- $5{:}47 \ \text{pm}$
- $3{:}42 \ \text{pm}$
- $2{:}56 \ \text{am}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What time is $1 \ \text{hour}$ $32 \ \text{minutes}$ before $7{:}19 \ \text{pm}$?
<emptyline>
- $4{:}42 \ \text{pm}$
- $4{:}52 \ \text{pm}$
- $5{:}47 \ \text{pm}$
- $3{:}42 \ \text{pm}$
- $2{:}56 \ \text{am}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

&= 5{:}47 \ \text {pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Kofi leaves for the shops at $11{:}23 \ \text{am}$ and returns $2 \ \text{hours}$ $48 \ \text{minutes}$ later. At what time does he return? 

- $1{:}01 \ \text{pm}$ 

- $1{:}11 \ \text{pm}$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Femi takes $2\ \text{hours}$ and $53\ \text{minutes}$ to cycle from London to Reading. If he arrived at $3{:}21 \ \text{pm}$, at what time must he have set off? 

A: $12{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ 

B: $1{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ 

C: $12{:}28 \ \text{pm}$ 

D: $12{:}18 \ \text{pm}$ 

E: $1{:}28 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
B: $1{:}24 \ \text{pm}$ 
<emptyline>
C: $12{:}28 \ \text{pm}$ 
<emptyline>
D: $12{:}18 \ \text{pm}$ 
<emptyline>
E: $1{:}28 \ \text{pm}$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Carla wants to record *Live* and *Kicking*. She thinks that the $180\ \text{minute}$ tape she has will be long enough. How many spare minutes will be left on the tape after recording *Live* and *Kicking*.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Carla wants to record *Live* and *Kicking*. She thinks that the $180\ \text{minute}$ tape she has will be long enough. How many spare minutes will be left on the tape after recording *Live* and *Kicking*.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                        &= 2 \ \text{hours} \ 55 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                        &= 175 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
\text {Spare minutes left}              &= 180 - 175 \\\\
                                        &= 5 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Thrse are the start and finish times on a DVD recorder:

START  $14{:}48$

FINISH $17{:}25$

For how long was the video recording? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Thrse are the start and finish times on a DVD recorder:
<emptyline>
START  $14{:}48$
<emptyline>
FINISH $17{:}25$
<emptyline>
For how long was the video recording? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Length of recording} &= 17{:}25 - 14{:}48 \\\\
                                  &= 2 \ \text {hours} \ 37 \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text {hours} \ 37 \ \text {minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is $463 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is $463 \ \text{minutes}$ in hours and minutes?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 463 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= \dfrac {463} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 7 \dfrac {43} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
&= 7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7 \ \text{hours} \ 43 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A boy has to be in school by $8.35 \ \text{am}$. It takes him $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to get washed and dressed, $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to eat breakfast and then $18 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk to school. What is the latest time that he should get out of bed?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A boy has to be in school by $8.35 \ \text{am}$. It takes him $25 \ \text{minutes}$ to get washed and dressed, $15 \ \text{minutes}$ to eat breakfast and then $18 \ \text{minutes}$ to walk to school. What is the latest time that he should get out of bed?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time}  &= 8{:}35  \ \text{am} - 25 \ \text{minutes} - 15 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 8{:}35  \ \text{am} - 58 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
             &= 7{:}37 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$7{:}37 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

You arrive at a bus stop $18 \ \text{minutes}$ late. The buses run every $45 \ \text{minutes}$. How long do you have to wait for the next bus? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">You arrive at a bus stop $18 \ \text{minutes}$ late. The buses run every $45 \ \text{minutes}$. How long do you have to wait for the next bus? 
</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting time} &= 45 \ \text{minutes} - 18 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                    &= 27 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Antony is making some cupcakes. It takes him $20 \ \text{minutes}$ to prepare the ingredients, the cakes take $22 \ \text{minutes}$ to bake and 
he needs to leave them for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to cool down. If he wants to eat them at $4{:}10 \ \text{pm}$, what time should he start preparing the ingredients?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Antony is making some cupcakes. It takes him $20 \ \text{minutes}$ to prepare the ingredients, the cakes take $22 \ \text{minutes}$ to bake and 
he needs to leave them for $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to cool down. If he wants to eat them at $4{:}10 \ \text{pm}$, what time should he start preparing the ingredients?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Start time}               &= 4{:}10 \ \text{pm}  - 20 \ \text{minutes} - 22 \ \text{minutes} - 10 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 4{:}10 \ \text{pm} - 52 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                &= 3{:}18 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3{:}18 \ \text{pm}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Subtract the number of $\text{hours}$ in a day from the number of $\text{seconds}$ in a $\text{minute}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Subtract the number of $\text{hours}$ in a day from the number of $\text{seconds}$ in a $\text{minute}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \text{Seconds in a minute} - \text{Hours in a day} \\\\
&= 60 - 24 \\\\
&= 36
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$36$

</div>
</div>

Tasweer is making some cupcakes. If she wants to eat them at $3{:}15 \ \text{pm}$, what time should she start preparing the ingredients? 

</div>

If she wants to eat them at $3{:}15 \ \text{pm}$, what time should she start preparing the ingredients? 
</code></pre>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many minutes are there in one day? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many minutes are there in one day? 
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many minutes are there from $11{:}11$ until $23{:}23$ on the same day?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 23{:}23 - 11{:}11 \\\\
&= 12 \ \text{hours} \ 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 12 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 12 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
&= 720 + 12 \\\\
&= 732 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$732 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Later, my clock shows

![papers/st-anselms-college/2018/sample-paper-3/s01-q07-s02-i00.png](/assets/papers/st-anselms-college/2018/sample-paper-3/s01-q07-s02-i00.png "papers/st-anselms-college/2018/sample-paper-3/s01-q07-s02-i00.png")

How many minutes will it be before the next time the hours and minutes are both multiples of $6 \text{?}$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Later, my clock shows
<emptyline>
%image%
<emptyline>
How many minutes will it be before the next time the hours and minutes are both multiples of $6 \text{?}$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The next hour multiple of $6$ is $12$.

The next minute multiple of $6$ after $58$ is $60$, meaning $00$ of the next hour.

So the next valid time is $12{:}00$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Minutes}  &= 12{:}00 - 11{:}58 \\\\
                &= 2 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I need to catch a bus from Wallasey Central Library to arrive at Upton Village by $11{:}30 \ \text{am}$. Which number bus should I catch and at what time? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I need to catch a bus from Wallasey Central Library to arrive at Upton Village by $11{:}30 \ \text{am}$. Which number bus should I catch and at what time? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

The arrival times at Upton Village are at every $27$ minutes past the hour starting from $10{:}27 \ \text{am}$.

The closest arrival time at Upton Village before $11{:}30 \ \text{am}$ is $11{:}27 \ \text{am}$ on the $\text{10A}$ bus.

From the timetable, the $\text{10A}$ bus arrives at Wallasey Central Library at $10{:}45 \ \text{am}$.

Answer $= \text{10A}$ bus at $10{:}45 \ \text{am}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\text{10A}$ bus at $10{:}45 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many minutes does the $10$A's journey from New Brighton to Noctorum take?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">

</div>
</div>

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken}     &= 10{:}34 - 09{:}39 \\\\
                       &= 55 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$55 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Time taken}     &= 10{:}34 - 09{:}39 \\\\
                       &= 55 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I need to catch a train from Birkenhead Park to arrive at Meols by $9.00$. What time train should I catch? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I need to catch a train from Birkenhead Park to arrive at Meols by $9.00$. What time train should I catch? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%

</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

From the timetable, 

Birkenhead Park times are: $8.14, 8.29, 8.44, 8.59$

Meols times are: $8.28$ and $8.58$

We choose the latest train that still arrives on time. So we should catch the $8.44$ train from Birkenhead Park, arriving Meols at $8.58$.

Answer $= 8{.}44$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A child's heart beats $80 \ \text{times per minute}$. How many times will it beat between $8{:}30\ \text{am}$ and $3{:}30\ \text{pm}$ on the same day? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A child's heart beats $80 \ \text{times per minute}$. How many times will it beat between $8{:}30\ \text{am}$ and $3{:}30\ \text{pm}$ on the same day? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total time}       &= 3 {:} 30 \ \text {pm} - 8 {:} 30 \ \text {am}\\\\
                         &= 7 \ \text {hours} \\\\
                         &= 7 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                         &= 420 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
\text {Heartbeat count}  &= 80 \times 420 \\\\
                         &= 33600
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$33600$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

She starts the swimming stage at $12{:}45 \ \text{pm}$. If she takes $79 \ \text{minutes}$ to complete this stage, at what time does she start the cycling stage.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">She starts the swimming stage at $12{:}45 \ \text{pm}$. If she takes $79 \ \text{minutes}$ to complete this stage, at what time does she start the cycling stage.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cycling start time}   &= 12{:}45 \ \text{pm} + 79 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                            &= 12{:}45 \ \text{pm} + 1 \ \text{hour } 19 \ \text{minutes}  \\\\
                            &=  2{:}04 \ \text{pm}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$2{:}04 \ \text{pm}$

</div>

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                    &= 154 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$

**Question**

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time taken} &=  79 \ \text{minutes} + 154 \ \text{minutes} + 107 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &=  340 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {340} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \dfrac {40} {60} \ \text{hours} \\\\
                        &= 5 \ \text{hours } 40 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Abigail gets up at $07{:}40$ in the morning. 

$25 \ \text{minutes}$ later she starts her breakfast, which takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to finish. 

She then leaves for school.  Her journey takes $35 \ \text{minutes}$ in total. 

She arrives $5 \ \text{minutes}$ late for registration. 

At what time is registration?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Abigail gets up at $07{:}40$ in the morning. 
<emptyline>
$25 \ \text{minutes}$ later she starts her breakfast, which takes $10 \ \text{minutes}$ to finish. 
<emptyline>
She then leaves for school.  Her journey takes $35 \ \text{minutes}$ in total. 
<emptyline>
She arrives $5 \ \text{minutes}$ late for registration. 
<emptyline>
At what time is registration?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Registration time}      &= 7{:}40 \ \text{am} + 25 \ \text{minutes} + 10 \ \text{minutes} + 35 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 8{:}05 \ \text{am} + 10 \ \text{minutes} + 35 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 8{:}15 \ \text{am} + 35 \ \text{minutes} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 8{:}50 \ \text{am} - 5 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                              &= 8{:}45 \ \text{am}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8{:}45 \ \text{am}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

My journey to school takes $47 \ \text{minutes}$. If I set off at $07{:}32$ what time will I arrive at school?

</div>

**Question**

What time do I get to school?

- $8.17 \ \text{am}$
- $08{:}16$
- $18{:}07$
- $18{:}17$
- $8.07 \ \text{am}$

**Question**

Jamie left school at $3{:}55 \ \text{pm}$ and arrived home $55 \ \text{minutes}$ later.

At what time did Jamie get home? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

                                 &= 4 {:} 50 \ \text {pm}

$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many days are there in $48 \ \text{weeks}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many days are there in $48 \ \text{weeks}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 48 \ \text{weeks} \\\\
&= 48 \times 7 \ \text{days} \\\\
&= 336 \ \text{days}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$336 \ \text{days}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A train leaves Stevenage at $0942$ and arrives in York at $1134$. How many **minutes** does the journey take?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A train leaves Stevenage at $0942$ and arrives in York at $1134$. How many **minutes** does the journey take?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time taken}           &= 11 {:} 34 - 09 {:} 42 \\\\
                            &= 1 \ \text {hour} \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 1 \times 60 \ \text {minutes} + \ 52 \ \text {minutes} \\\\
                            &= 60 + 52 \\\\
                            &= 112  \ \text {minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$112 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The opening hours of my local supermarket are $7 \ \text{am}$ to $9 \ \text{pm}$ Mondays to Saturdays and 
$10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ to $4{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ on Sundays. For how many hours does this supermarket open each week?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The opening hours of my local supermarket are $7 \ \text{am}$ to $9 \ \text{pm}$ Mondays to Saturdays and 
$10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ to $4{:}30 \ \text{pm}$ on Sundays. For how many hours does this supermarket open each week?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total hours}    &= 6 \times ( 9 \ \text{pm} - 7 \ \text{am} ) + (04{:}30 \ \text{pm} - 10{:}30 \ \text{am}) \\\\
                      &= 6 \times 14 \ \text{hours} +  6 \ \text{hours} \\\\
                      &= 84 + 6 \\\\
                      &= 90 \ \text{hours}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$90 \ \text {hours}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
