<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A concert performance of Mahler's third symphony begins at $7{:}35 \ \text{pm}$ and ends at 
$9{:}11 \ \text{pm}$. 
How many minutes does it last?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">A concert performance of Mahler's third symphony begins at $7{:}35 \ \text{pm}$ and ends at 
$9{:}11 \ \text{pm}$. 
How many minutes does it last?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Duration}            &= 9{:}11 \ \text{pm} - 7{:}35 \ \text{pm} \\\\
                           &= 1 \ \text{hour} \ 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &= 1 \times 60 \ \text{minutes} + 36 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                           &=60 + 36 \\\\
                           &= 96 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$96 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Dita watches the television for $2 \text{ hours } 45 \text{ minutes}$ each weekend. How much time does she spend watching television over three weekends?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Dita watches the television for $2 \text{ hours } 45 \text{ minutes}$ each weekend. How much time does she spend watching television over three weekends?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total time} &= 3 \times 2 \ \text{hours} \ 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 3 \times 2 \ \text{hours} + 3 \times 45 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 6 \ \text{hours} + 135 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 6 \ \text{hours} + 2 \ \text{hours} + 15 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                  &= 8 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$8 \ \text{hours} \ 15 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>

ttime

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

I arrived at the station at $7.47 \ \text{a.m}$. My train was due at five past eight but was $13 \ \text{minutes}$ late.

How long did I have to wait for my train?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">I arrived at the station at $7.47 \ \text{a.m}$. My train was due at five past eight but was $13 \ \text{minutes}$ late.
<emptyline>
How long did I have to wait for my train?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Waiting time} &= 8{:}05 \ \text{am} + 13 \ \text{minutes} - 7{:}47 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 8{:}18 \ \text{am} - 7{:}47 \ \text{am} \\\\
                    &= 31 \ \text{minutes}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$31 \ \text{minutes}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
